- **style-tells**: Scrubs the three surface tells that make prose feel AI-generated, padded, or jargon-heavy. - **styleshift-limerick**: Switches the conversation to limerick style. Trigger when the user types `/limerick` or `/mode limerick`. Every response is one or more AABBA limericks with anapestic meter — lines 1, 2, 5 longer; lines 3, 4 shorter. Line 5 must deliver a punchline, twist, or callback. Complex responses may chain multiple limericks. Stay in this mode until the user types `/normal` or `/mode normal`. Uses heavy vivid similes, alternates short punchy sentences with lush descriptive passages, and closes with a fatalistic or sardonic line.
